`ports`:
`ports` is used to set the published port numbers for a container. Each `service` within a `docker-compose.yml` file can have one or more `ports` specified. When using the Docker CLI or Docker Compose to run a container, you can map the `ports` from the host system to the container's `ports` using the `-p` or `--publish` flag. The format is `-p <host_port>:<container_port>` or `--publish
```

**Response B**

```
In Docker Compose, both the Expose and Ports sections define how external services are to be accessed. The Expose section specifies which ports are to be exposed on the container. The Ports section specifies the host port that the container is to listen to. The difference between the two is that the Expose section is used to expose a port on the container to the host, while the Ports section is used to map a host port to a container port.
```
